([Getty/p5-file-sops](https://github.com/Getty/p5-file-sops/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/file-sops-api.md) · ★1)
- **file-sops-format**: File::SOPS format-handler worker — the YAML and JSON parsers and emitters, the order-preserving YAML::PP reparse, quoting and boolean handling, multi-document streams. Beware: emitter choice and the MAC are one mechanism, so work here reaches into the wire layer more often than it looks. Use for parser/emitter changes and format-specific interop failures. ([Getty/p5-file-sops](https://github.com/Getty/p5-file-sops/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/file-sops-format.md) · ★1)

Release audit: File::SOPS before release — Changes/{{$NEXT}} current, cpanfile complete and Getty-authored deps pinned to latest CPAN, dist.ini [@Author::GETTY] sane, dzil build clean, and the interop suite actually executed rather than skipped. Workers never commit; this agent does. Never pushes, tags or releases. ([Getty/p5-file-sops](https://github.com/Getty/p5-file-sops/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/file-sops-release-manager.md) · ★1)

- **file-sops-wire**: File::SOPS wire-format and crypto worker — value types, the value→bytes conversion, character encoding, AES-GCM, the MAC and its AAD, and the encryption backends.
